    """
    backend/merge_finetuned_models.py
    ===============================================================================
    Zero-HuggingFace, Pure-NumPy In-Place GGUF LoRA Merger for `batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4`
    ===============================================================================
    1. Extracts `coding_ft.zip` (`scicompute_ft_data/gemma_e2b_best_loss_adapter`)
       and `physchem_ft.zip` (`physchem_ft_outputs/gemma_e2b_physchem_best_adapter`).
    2. Blends all 410 LoRA tensors (35% Coding + 65% PhysChem) into
       `ChatBot/merged_ft_adapter/adapter_model.safetensors`.
    3. Queries `ollama show --modelfile batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4` to find the exact local
       GGUF blob (`C:\\Users\\harsh\\.ollama\\models\\blobs\\sha256-...`) pulled from Ollama.
    4. Copies that GGUF blob to `merged_ft_adapter/batiai_gemma4_e2b_ft_merged.gguf` and
       patches all 205 transformer layer matrices (`blk.{L}.attn_q/k/v/output`,
       `blk.{L}.ffn_gate/up/down`) in-place across Q4_0, Q4_1, Q8_0, Q4_K, Q5_K, Q6_K,
       F16, BF16, and F32 quantization blocks using `Delta_W = (lora_alpha / sqrt(r)) * (B @ A)`.
    5. Re-registers the patched GGUF directly as `batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4` in Ollama so the
       entire ChatBot UI (`backend/config.py`, `frontend/index.html`, `frontend/app.js`,
       `SingleShotAgent`, `AgenticRAGAgent`, and `pytest`) uses the merged weights automatically.
    """

    import struct
    import shutil
    import zipfile
    import subprocess
    from pathlib import Path
    from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple, List
    
    import numpy as np
    
    REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "merged_ft_adapter"
    PATCHED_GGUF_PATH = MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "batiai_gemma4_e2b_ft_merged.gguf"
    ORIG_MODELFILE_BACKUP = MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "original_batiai_modelfile.txt"
    TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL = "batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4"
    
    UNIFIED_SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
        "You are SciCompute PhysChem & Code AI, a principal computational materials physicist, quantum chemist, "
        "and scientific Python engineer specialized in ASE, Quantum ESPRESSO (DFT), DeePMD-kit, and LAMMPS.\n"
        "1. Chemical Bonding & Phase Partitioning: Intramolecular covalent O-H bonds in H2O/OH* are ~0.97 Å (cutoff r_OH = 1.25 Å), "
        "whereas intermolecular H-bonds start at >=1.65 Å. Always partition water oxygens (`O_w`) from crystal lattice oxygens (`O_slab`) "
        "via `neighbor_list('ij', atoms, cutoff={('O', 'H'): 1.25})` BEFORE computing crystal surface layers or enclosing boundary shells (r_if = 3.0-3.2 Å under PBC).\n"
        "2. Statistical Mechanics & Units: Planar water mass density rho(z) [g/cm^3] = ((N_Ow / 6.02214076e23) * 18.01528) / (A_xy * dz * 1e-24). "
        "Normalize partial RDFs g_AB(r) by ideal-gas spherical shell count `(N_A * N_B / V_cell) * 4 * pi * r^2 * dr` under MIC (`mic=True`).\n"
        "3. ASE API Contracts: `neighbor_list('ijd', atoms, cutoff)` returns three 1D arrays `(nl_i, nl_j, nl_d)`—never index as 2D `d[i,j]`. "
        "Neither `Atoms` nor `Atom` has `.get_neighbors()`. Use plain Unicode ('TiO2', 'Å') and never `\\text{}` in plot labels."
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. Locate `batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4` Local GGUF Blob & Patch In-Place
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def find_ollama_gguf_blob_and_modelfile(model_name: str = TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL) -> Tuple[Optional[Path], str]:
        MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        modelfile_text = ""
        if ORIG_MODELFILE_BACKUP.exists():
            modelfile_text = ORIG_MODELFILE_BACKUP.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        else:
            try:
                res = subprocess.run(["ollama", "show", "--modelfile", model_name], capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
                if res.returncode == 0 and res.stdout.strip():
                    modelfile_text = res.stdout
                    if "batiai_gemma4_e2b_ft_merged.gguf" not in modelfile_text:
                        ORIG_MODELFILE_BACKUP.write_text(modelfile_text, encoding="utf-8")
            except FileNotFoundError:
                pass
    
        candidates: List[Path] = []
        for line in modelfile_text.splitlines():
            s = line.strip()
            if s.upper().startswith("FROM "):
                p_str = s[5:].strip().strip('"').strip("'")
                p = Path(p_str)
                if p.exists() and p.is_file() and p.resolve() != PATCHED_GGUF_PATH.resolve():
                    candidates.append(p)
    
        ollama_blobs_dir = Path.home() / ".ollama" / "models" / "blobs"
        if not candidates and ollama_blobs_dir.exists():
            for blob in sorted(ollama_blobs_dir.glob("sha256-*"), key=lambda x: x.stat().st_size, reverse=True):
                if blob.stat().st_size > 500_000_000:
                    candidates.append(blob)
    
        for cand in candidates:
            try:
                _, t_map = parse_gguf_tensors(cand)
                if any("attn_q.weight" in k or "q_proj.weight" in k for k in t_map):
        modelfile_patched = MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "Modelfile.batiai_patched"
        modelfile_patched.write_text("\n".join(out_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return modelfile_patched
    
    
    def register_unified_model_in_ollama(
        target_model_name: str = TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        base_ollama_model: str = TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        alpha_coding: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        coding_dir, physchem_dir = locate_coding_and_physchem_adapters(REPO_ROOT)
        if not coding_dir or not physchem_dir:
            return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "coding_ft.zip or physchem_ft.zip adapters not found."}
    
        manifest = blend_lora_adapters(coding_dir, physchem_dir, MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR, alpha_coding=alpha_coding)
        base_blob, orig_modelfile = find_ollama_gguf_blob_and_modelfile(base_ollama_model)
    
        if base_blob is not None:
            patch_stats = patch_ollama_gguf_with_blended_lora(base_blob, MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR, PATCHED_GGUF_PATH)
            manifest.update(patch_stats)
            modelfile_path = build_patched_modelfile(orig_modelfile, base_blob, PATCHED_GGUF_PATH)
    
            res = subprocess.run(["ollama", "create", target_model_name, "-f", str(modelfile_path)], capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
            if res.returncode == 0:
                print(f"[FT Merger] Successfully updated `{target_model_name}` in Ollama with in-place LoRA-patched GGUF weights!")
                return {
                    "status": "success",
                    "mode": "in_place_gguf_lora_merged",
                    "active_model": target_model_name,
                    "manifest": manifest,
                }
            return {"status": "error", "error": res.stderr.strip(), "manifest": manifest}
    
        return {"status": "merged_adapter_only", "active_model": target_model_name, "manifest": manifest}
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        print(json.dumps(register_unified_model_in_ollama(), indent=2))

    import os
    from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict
    
    class Settings(BaseSettings):
        model_config = SettingsConfigDict(env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")
    
        OLLAMA_BASE_URL: str = os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:11434")
        MODEL_NAME: str = os.getenv("MODEL_NAME", "batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4")

    from pydantic import BaseModel
    from backend.config import settings
    import uuid

    from backend.agents.execution_intent import requests_sandbox_execution
    from backend.agents.agentic_agent import AgenticRAGAgent

    def _index_evaluated_memory(session_id, prompt, response, evaluation):
        if session_id is None or evaluation.get("audit_status") == "error" or (evaluation.get("score") or 0) < 80:
            return
        try:
            from backend.rag.chat_memory import get_chat_memory_store
            get_chat_memory_store().index_turn(
                session_id=session_id, user_message=prompt, assistant_message=response,
                agent_used="SingleShotAgent", execution_required=True,
                evaluation_score=evaluation["score"],
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"Notice: Could not index evaluated memory: {exc}")
    
    
    @asynccontextmanager
    async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
        init_db()
        try:
            from backend.rag.ingest_dataset_zip import ingest_configured_dataset
            await asyncio.to_thread(ingest_configured_dataset)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"Notice: Curated dataset sync failed: {exc}")
        try:
            from backend.rag.chat_memory import get_chat_memory_store
            get_chat_memory_store().ingest_all_sessions()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Notice: Background memory sync on startup skipped: {e}")
    
        settings.NUM_GPU = int(os.getenv("NUM_GPU", str(settings.NUM_GPU)))
        gpu_mode = "CPU-only (NUM_GPU=0)" if settings.NUM_GPU == 0 else (
            "GPU — all layers (AMD Radeon via ROCm/HIP)" if settings.NUM_GPU == -1
            else f"GPU — {settings.NUM_GPU} layers"
        )
        try:
            from backend.merge_finetuned_models import register_unified_model_in_ollama, UNIFIED_MODEL_NAME
            merge_res = register_unified_model_in_ollama(base_ollama_model=settings.MODEL_NAME)
            if merge_res.get("status") == "success":
                settings.MODEL_NAME = merge_res.get("active_model", UNIFIED_MODEL_NAME)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Notice: Fine-tuned adapter auto-merge skipped: {e}")
        print(f"Starting Scientific Computing API with active model: {settings.MODEL_NAME}")
        print(f"Inference device: {gpu_mode}")
        print(f"  To force CPU-only: set NUM_GPU=0 before starting (e.g. NUM_GPU=0 uvicorn ...)")
        print("Zero-Shot Script Generation & Accuracy Evaluation Engine active")
        try:
            yield
        except (asyncio.CancelledError, KeyboardInterrupt):
            pass
    
    async def startup_event():
        """Backward-compatible helper for tests invoking startup_event directly."""
        init_db()
        try:
            from backend.rag.ingest_dataset_zip import ingest_configured_dataset
            await asyncio.to_thread(ingest_configured_dataset)
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"Notice: Curated dataset sync failed: {exc}")
        try:
            from backend.rag.chat_memory import get_chat_memory_store
            get_chat_memory_store().ingest_all_sessions()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Notice: Background memory sync on startup skipped: {e}")
    
    app = FastAPI(title="Scientific Computing Chatbox API", version="1.0.0", lifespan=lifespan)
    
    # Setup frontend directory (located at root ChatBot/frontend)
    FRONTEND_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "frontend"
    FRONTEND_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=str(FRONTEND_DIR)), name="static")
    
    PUBLIC_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "public"
    PUBLIC_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    app.mount("/public", StaticFiles(directory=str(PUBLIC_DIR)), name="public")
    
    class ChatRequest(BaseModel):
        message: str
        session_id: Optional[int] = None
        system_prompt: Optional[str] = None
        mode: Optional[str] = "single_shot"
        include_notebook_context: Optional[bool] = None
        notebook_name: Optional[str] = "running.ipynb"
        images: Optional[List[str]] = None
        run_sandbox: Optional[bool] = None
    
    class SessionCreateRequest(BaseModel):
        title: Optional[str] = "New Session"
    
    class EvaluationRequest(BaseModel):
        session_id: Optional[int] = None
        prompt: Optional[str] = None
        notebook_name: Optional[str] = "running.ipynb"
        include_notebook_context: Optional[bool] = True
    
    class GeminiEvaluationRequest(BaseModel):
        code: str
        prompt: Optional[str] = None
        execution_output: Optional[str] = None
        session_id: Optional[int] = None
    
    class NotebookAttachRequest(BaseModel):
        code: Optional[str] = None
        notebook_name: Optional[str] = "running.ipynb"
        replace_placeholder: Optional[bool] = True
    
    class PromptCreateRequest(BaseModel):
        id: Optional[str] = None
        title: str
        domain: str
        prompt: str
    
    class PromptUpdateRequest(BaseModel):
        title: Optional[str] = None
        domain: Optional[str] = None
        prompt: Optional[str] = None
    
    @app.get("/")
    def read_root():
        index_path = FRONTEND_DIR / "index.html"
        if index_path.exists():
            return FileResponse(str(index_path))
        return {"status": "ok", "message": "Scientific Computing Chatbox API is running"}
    
    @app.get("/api/model_info")
    def get_model_info():
        """Returns active model, GPU mode, and baseline evaluation status"""
        settings.NUM_GPU = int(os.getenv("NUM_GPU", str(settings.NUM_GPU)))
        gpu_mode = "CPU-only" if settings.NUM_GPU == 0 else (
            f"GPU (all layers)" if settings.NUM_GPU == -1 else f"GPU ({settings.NUM_GPU} layers)"
        )
        return {
            "model_name": settings.MODEL_NAME,
            "ollama_base_url": settings.OLLAMA_BASE_URL,
            "mode": "single_shot",
            "tools_enabled": False,
            "rag_enabled": False,
            "gpu_mode": gpu_mode,
            "num_gpu": settings.NUM_GPU,
            "description": "Pure Single-Shot Prompt Evaluation Mode (Zero Tools / Zero RAG)"
        }
    
    @app.get("/api/benchmark_prompts")
    def get_benchmark_prompts():
        """Curated domain prompts to benchmark Gemma4 single-shot accuracy"""
        return get_prompts()

    def create_benchmark_prompt(request: PromptCreateRequest):
        """Create a new benchmark or custom evaluation prompt"""
        title = request.title.strip()
        domain = request.domain.strip()
        prompt_text = request.prompt.strip()

    import re
    import requests
    from backend.config import settings
    from backend.rag.hybrid_retriever import HybridRetriever
    from backend.tools.web_tools import search_web, read_url_content
    
    QA_SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
        "SYSTEM DIRECTIVE: You are SciCompute AI, an authoritative scientific computing and computational materials assistant "
        "specialized in Density Functional Theory (DFT), Molecular Dynamics (LAMMPS), Machine Learning Potentials (DeepMD-kit), and ASE.\n"
        "\n"
        "CORE PROTOCOLS:\n"
        "1. Grounding & Provenance: Ground your answer strictly on the provided Hybrid RAG context (ChromaDB vector documents and Kùzu knowledge graph relationships). "
        "Prioritize retrieved parameters, formulas, cutoffs, and data structures.\n"
        "2. Context Boundaries: If the context contains the answer, synthesize it directly with high technical precision. "
        "If the question exceeds the context, leverage your computational materials science knowledge but state this boundary concisely.\n"
        "3. Mathematical Precision: Formulate all physical equations and parameters using standard LaTeX ($...$ for inline, $$...$$ for block display).\n"
        "4. Concise Execution: Get straight to the point and do not beat around the bush. Provide zero introductory pleasantries, filler phrases, or conversational preambles. "
        "Deliver the direct answer followed by rigorous technical elaboration."
    )
    
    class QAAgent:
        def __init__(self):
            self.retriever = HybridRetriever()
            self.model = settings.MODEL_NAME
            self.base_url = settings.OLLAMA_BASE_URL
    
        def search_web(self, query: str, max_results: int = 3) -> list:
            """Searches the web via DuckDuckGo."""
            return search_web(query, max_results=max_results)
    
        def read_url_content(self, url: str) -> str:
            """Fetches and extracts text from a specified URL."""
            return read_url_content(url)
    
        def answer_question(self, question: str) -> str:
            """Alias for answer() method."""
            return self.answer(question)
    
        def answer(self, question: str) -> str:
            # 1. Retrieve context
            print("Retrieving context...")
            context = self.retriever.retrieve(question)
    
            # 2. Enrich with web tools if requested or URLs present
            web_context = []
            if re.search(r"\b(search (?:the )?web|web search|look up online|search online)\b", question, re.I):
                clean_q = re.sub(r"(?i)\b(search (?:the )?web (?:for)?|look up online (?:for)?|web search)\b", "", question).strip() or question
                web_hits = self.search_web(clean_q, max_results=3)
                if web_hits and not web_hits[0].get("error"):
                    formatted = "\n".join(f"- {h.get('title')}: {h.get('body')} ({h.get('href')})" for h in web_hits if h.get("title"))
                    if formatted:
                        web_context.append(f"Web Search Results:\n{formatted}")
    
            urls = re.findall(r"https?://[^\s)\]]+", question)
            for u in urls[:2]:
                u_text = self.read_url_content(u)
                if u_text and not u_text.startswith("Error"):
                    web_context.append(f"Content from {u}:\n{u_text[:2000]}")
    
            if web_context:
                context = f"{context}\n\n" + "\n\n".join(web_context)
    
            # 2. Build user prompt
            user_prompt = f"""Retrieved Domain Context:
    {context}
    
    Question:
    {question}
    
    Provide an authoritative, direct scientific answer:"""
    
            # 3. Call LLM with native system role and options
            print(f"Calling Ollama ({self.model})...")
            messages = [
                {"role": "system", "content": QA_SYSTEM_PROMPT},
                {"role": "user", "content": user_prompt}
            ]
            try:
                response = requests.post(
                    f"{self.base_url}/api/chat",
                    json={
                        "model": self.model,
                        "messages": messages,
                        "stream": False,
                        "options": {
                            "num_ctx": settings.NUM_CTX,
                            "temperature": 0.2,
                            "num_gpu": settings.NUM_GPU,
                        }
                    },
                    timeout=120
                )
                response.raise_for_status()
                data = response.json()
                if "message" in data:
                    return data["message"].get("content", "")
                return data.get("response", "")
            except Exception as e:
                # Fallback to /api/generate for backward compatibility or unit test mocks
                try:
                    gen_payload = {
                        "model": self.model,
                        "system": QA_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
                        "prompt": f"{QA_SYSTEM_PROMPT}\n\n{user_prompt}",
                        "stream": False
                    }
                    fallback_res = requests.post(
                        f"{self.base_url}/api/generate",
                        json=gen_payload,
                        timeout=120
                    )
                    fallback_res.raise_for_status()
                    fdata = fallback_res.json()
                    return fdata.get("response") or fdata.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
                except Exception:
                    return f"Error connecting to LLM: {e}"
    
    if __name__ == "__main__": # pragma: no cover
        import sys
        agent = QAAgent()
        print("=" * 72)
        print("📚 Hybrid RAG Scientific Q&A Agent CLI")
        print(f"Model: {agent.model} | Endpoint: {agent.base_url}")
        print("Capabilities: ChromaDB Vector Store + Kùzu Knowledge Graph")
        print("Type your scientific question below. Type 'exit', 'quit', or 'q' to end.")
        print("=" * 72)
        print()

    ANALYSIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
        "SYSTEM DIRECTIVE: You are an expert computational materials scientist and autonomous Python developer "
        "operating inside an isolated E2B / Docker execution sandbox (chatbox-sandbox:latest).\n"
        "Your objective is to generate self-contained, robust, executable scientific Python scripts that run flawlessly on the first execution.\n"
        "\n"
        "EXECUTION & SANDBOX ENVIRONMENT RULES:\n"
        "1. Available packages: ASE (Atomic Simulation Environment 3.29.0), NumPy, SciPy, Matplotlib, Pandas.\n"
        "2. Data Grounding & ASE Syntax: Never assume variables are pre-loaded in memory. Always read trajectories directly using ASE `read(data_path, index=':')`.\n"
        "   CRITICAL: `read(data_path, index=':')` returns a Python list of Atoms objects (`list[ase.Atoms]`). "
        "A Python `list` does NOT have `.get_positions()` or `.cell` attributes! "
        "Always unpack or index: `traj = read(data_path, index=':'); atoms = traj[0] if isinstance(traj, list) else traj; positions = atoms.get_positions()`. "
        "Or read single frame: `atoms = read(data_path, index=0)`. Verify file existence and handle frames cleanly.\n"
        "3. Coordinate Filtering & Boundary Layer Analysis:\n"
        "   - NEVER use exact floating-point equality (`==`) on continuous coordinates (e.g. `positions[:, 2] == z_min` will collapse an entire atomic plane to 1 or 2 isolated points!).\n"
        "   - Slab Surface Boundaries along z: Always use a physical layer thickness tolerance window `tol` (0.5 to 1.0 Å). Identify surface layers via `positions[:, 2] <= (z_min + tol)` or `positions[:, 2] >= (z_max - tol)`.\n"
        "   - 3D Unit Cell Box Boundaries: Check fractional / scaled coordinates `scaled = atoms.get_scaled_positions(wrap=True)` within a skin depth `delta` (e.g. 0.02 to 0.05) across cell faces: `np.any((scaled < delta) | (scaled > 1.0 - delta), axis=1)`.\n"
        "   - Always print the count, chemical symbols, and coordinate arrays of the identified boundary atoms for verification.\n"
        "4. Interfacial Analysis: For neighbor lists, coordination, and cutoffs, use `ase.neighborlist` (e.g. `neighbor_list`, `natural_cutoffs`, or `NeighborList`).\n"
        "5. Headless Visualization: Save all plots to the current working directory as PNG files (`plt.savefig(..., dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')`). "
        "Always call `plt.close()` after saving. NEVER call `plt.show()` (hangs headless containers).\n"
        "6. Output Contract: Output ONLY the complete, valid Python code block enclosed in ```python ... ```. "
        "Get straight to the point and do not beat around the bush. Eliminate conversational filler, explanations, or preambles."
    )
    
    ANALYSIS_FIX_SYSTEM_PROMPT = (
        "SYSTEM DIRECTIVE: You are an autonomous scientific code debugger and self-correction engine for the E2B / Docker execution sandbox.\n"
        "A previously generated script failed execution in the sandbox.\n"
        "1. Traceback Isolation: Identify the exact line and root cause in the stderr traceback.\n"
        "   - If 'AttributeError: 'list' object has no attribute 'get_positions'', unpack the ASE trajectory list: `atoms = traj[0] if isinstance(traj, list) else traj`.\n"
        "   - If boundary analysis returned only 1-2 atoms, replace exact float comparison `== z_min` with a physical layer tolerance `z <= z_min + tol` (e.g. tol = 0.8 Å) or fractional coordinates.\n"
        "2. Solution Synthesis: Apply targeted corrections using error diagnostics and web search context without regressing existing imports or functional logic.\n"
        "3. Output Contract: Output ONLY the complete, corrected, executable Python code in a ```python ... ``` block. "
        "Do not provide explanations or conversational apologies."
    )
    
    class AnalysisAgent:
        def __init__(self):
            self.retriever = HybridRetriever()
            self.model = settings.MODEL_NAME
            self.base_url = settings.OLLAMA_BASE_URL
    
        def search_web(self, query: str, max_results: int = 3) -> list:
            """Searches the web via DuckDuckGo."""
            return search_web(query, max_results=max_results)
    
        def read_url_content(self, url: str) -> str:
            """Fetches and extracts text from a specified URL."""
            return read_url_content(url)
    
        def _call_llm(self, prompt: str, system_prompt: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
            try:
                messages = []
                if system_prompt:
                    messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system_prompt})
                messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt})
    
                payload = {
                    "model": self.model,
                    "messages": messages,
                    "stream": False,
                    "options": {
                        "num_ctx": settings.NUM_CTX,
                        "temperature": 0.1,
                        "num_gpu": settings.NUM_GPU,
                    }
                }
                response = requests.post(
                    f"{self.base_url}/api/chat",
                    json=payload,
                    timeout=120
                )
                response.raise_for_status()
                data = response.json()
                if "message" in data:
                    return data["message"].get("content", "")
                return data.get("response", "")
            except Exception as e:
                # Fallback to /api/generate for backward compatibility or unit test mocks
                try:
                    gen_payload = {
                        "model": self.model,
                        "prompt": f"{system_prompt}\n\n{prompt}" if system_prompt else prompt,
                        "stream": False
                    }
                    fallback_res = requests.post(
                        f"{self.base_url}/api/generate",
                        json=gen_payload,
                        timeout=120
                    )
                    fallback_res.raise_for_status()
                    fdata = fallback_res.json()
                    return fdata.get("response") or fdata.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
                except Exception:
                    return f"Error connecting to LLM: {e}"

                        "status": "success",

    import pytest
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    from backend.main import app
    
    client = TestClient(app)
    
    def test_read_root():
        response = client.get("/")
        assert response.status_code == 200
    
    def test_get_model_info():
        response = client.get("/api/model_info")
        assert response.status_code == 200
        data = response.json()
        assert data["mode"] == "single_shot"
        assert data["tools_enabled"] is False
        assert data["rag_enabled"] is False
    
    def test_get_benchmark_prompts():
        response = client.get("/api/benchmark_prompts")
        assert response.status_code == 200
        prompts = response.json()
        assert len(prompts) == 5
        domains = [p["domain"] for p in prompts]
        assert "DFT" in domains
        assert "Molecular Dynamics" in domains
        assert "Interfacial Script Evaluation" in domains
    
    def test_chat_endpoint_success(mocker):
        mock_chat_res = {
            "status": "success",
            "response": "Platinum has an fcc structure.",
            "model": "batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4",
            "latency_seconds": 0.42,
            "session_id": 99
        }
        mocker.patch("backend.main.SingleShotAgent.chat", return_value=mock_chat_res)
    
        payload = {"message": "Describe Pt crystal structure"}
        response = client.post("/api/chat", json=payload)
        assert response.status_code == 200
        data = response.json()
        assert data["status"] == "success"
        assert "Platinum" in data["response"]
        assert data["session_id"] == 99
    
    def test_chat_endpoint_empty_message():
        response = client.post("/api/chat", json={"message": "   "})
        assert response.status_code == 400
        assert "Message cannot be empty" in response.json()["detail"]
    
    def test_chat_stream_endpoint_success(mocker):
        from backend.agents.baseline_agent import SingleShotAgent
        def fake_stream(*args, **kwargs):
            yield 'data: {"type": "session", "session_id": 10}\n\n'
            yield 'data: {"type": "content", "delta": "Hello"}\n\n'
            yield 'data: {"type": "done", "latency_seconds": 0.5}\n\n'
    
        mocker.patch.object(SingleShotAgent, "chat_stream", side_effect=fake_stream)
    
        response = client.post("/api/chat/stream", json={"message": "Hello stream"})
        assert response.status_code == 200
        assert "text/event-stream" in response.headers["content-type"]
        body = response.text
        assert "Hello" in body
        assert "session" in body
    
    def test_chat_stream_endpoint_empty_message():
        response = client.post("/api/chat/stream", json={"message": ""})
        assert response.status_code == 400
        assert "Message cannot be empty" in response.json()["detail"]

    """Initialize the registered finetuned model without rebuilding cached weights."""
    
    import requests
    
    from backend.config import settings
    from backend.merge_finetuned_models import (
        TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        register_unified_model_in_ollama,
    )
    
    
    def initialize_finetuned_model():
        result = register_unified_model_in_ollama(
            target_model_name=TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
            base_ollama_model=settings.MODEL_NAME,
        )
        if result.get("status") != "success":
            raise RuntimeError(f"Finetuned model initialization failed: {result}")
    
        # An empty chat loads the existing registered weights without generating text.
        # Match inference options so the first request can reuse the loaded runner.
        response = requests.post(
            f"{settings.OLLAMA_BASE_URL.rstrip('/')}/api/chat",
            json={
                "model": result["active_model"],
                "messages": [],
                "stream": False,
                "keep_alive": settings.OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE,
                "options": {"num_ctx": settings.NUM_CTX, "num_gpu": settings.NUM_GPU},
            },
            timeout=300,
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
        data = response.json()
        if data.get("error") or not data.get("done"):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Finetuned model preload failed: {data}")
        settings.MODEL_NAME = result["active_model"]
        return result

        from pathlib import Path
        for name in ('baseline_agent.py', 'agentic_agent.py', 'qa_agent.py', 'analysis_agent.py'):
            path = Path('backend/agents') / name
            with path.open(encoding='utf-8', newline='') as handle:
                source = handle.read()
            lines = source.splitlines(keepends=True)
            output = []
            for line in lines:
                output.append(line)
                if '"model": self.model,' in line:
                    indent = line[:len(line) - len(line.lstrip())]
                    newline = '\r\n' if line.endswith('\r\n') else '\n'
                    # Only inference payloads, not result metadata.
                    following = lines[len(output):] if False else []
                    output.append(indent + '"keep_alive": settings.OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE,' + newline)
            # Remove additions in result dictionaries; keep them only in request payloads below.
            updated = ''.join(output)
            import ast
            tree = ast.parse(updated)
            remove_lines = set()
            for node in ast.walk(tree):
                if isinstance(node, ast.Dict):
                    keys = {key.value for key in node.keys if isinstance(key, ast.Constant)}
                    if 'keep_alive' in keys and not ({'messages', 'prompt'} & keys):
                        for key in node.keys:
                            if isinstance(key, ast.Constant) and key.value == 'keep_alive':
                                remove_lines.add(key.lineno)
            updated = ''.join(line for index, line in enumerate(updated.splitlines(keepends=True), 1) if index not in remove_lines)
            with path.open('w', encoding='utf-8', newline='') as handle:
                handle.write(updated)
            print(name, updated.count('"keep_alive": settings.OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE'))

            for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b''):
                digest.update(chunk)
        expected = 'sha256-' + digest.hexdigest()
        assert Path(blob).name == expected, 'Registered model does not match merged GGUF'
        print('Verified: Ollama registered blob matches merged GGUF SHA-256: ' + digest.hexdigest())
        '@ | python -

    from unittest.mock import Mock
    
    import pytest
    import requests
    
    from backend import model_runtime
    from backend import merge_finetuned_models as merger
    
    
    def test_cached_startup_never_merges_or_registers_again(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
        gguf = tmp_path / "merged.gguf"
        gguf.write_bytes(b"GGUF")
        (tmp_path / "merge_manifest.json").write_text('{"total_tensors": 410}', encoding="utf-8")
        monkeypatch.setattr(merger, "PATCHED_GGUF_PATH", gguf)
        monkeypatch.setattr(merger, "MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR", tmp_path)
        locate = Mock(side_effect=AssertionError("Cached startup must not rebuild"))
        run = Mock(side_effect=AssertionError("Cached startup must not call ollama create"))
        monkeypatch.setattr(merger, "locate_coding_and_physchem_adapters", locate)
        monkeypatch.setattr(merger.subprocess, "run", run)
    
        for _ in range(2):
            result = merger.register_unified_model_in_ollama()
            assert result["mode"] == "cached_in_place_gguf"
            assert result["active_model"] == merger.TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL
        locate.assert_not_called()
        run.assert_not_called()
    
    
    def test_startup_preloads_finetuned_tag_with_persistent_runner(monkeypatch):
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime.settings, "MODEL_NAME", "base-model")
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime.settings, "OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE", -1)
        register = Mock(return_value={"status": "success", "active_model": merger.TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL})
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime, "register_unified_model_in_ollama", register)
        response = Mock()
        response.json.return_value = {"done": True}
        post = Mock(return_value=response)
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime.requests, "post", post)
    
        model_runtime.initialize_finetuned_model()
    
        assert model_runtime.settings.MODEL_NAME == merger.TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL
        payload = post.call_args.kwargs["json"]
        assert payload["model"] == merger.TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL
        assert payload["messages"] == []
        assert payload["keep_alive"] == -1
        assert payload["options"] == {
            "num_ctx": model_runtime.settings.NUM_CTX,
            "num_gpu": model_runtime.settings.NUM_GPU,
        }
        response.raise_for_status.assert_called_once()
    
    
    def test_startup_does_not_silently_fall_back_to_base(monkeypatch):
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime, "register_unified_model_in_ollama", Mock(return_value={"status": "skipped"}))
        post = Mock()
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime.requests, "post", post)
        with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="initialization failed"):
            model_runtime.initialize_finetuned_model()
        post.assert_not_called()
    
    
    def test_startup_fails_if_registered_model_cannot_load(monkeypatch):
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime, "register_unified_model_in_ollama", Mock(return_value={
            "status": "success", "active_model": merger.TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        }))
        response = Mock()
        response.raise_for_status.side_effect = requests.HTTPError("Model not found")
        monkeypatch.setattr(model_runtime.requests, "post", Mock(return_value=response))
        with pytest.raises(requests.HTTPError, match="Model not found"):
            model_runtime.initialize_finetuned_model()

## Assistant

Done. The app now preloads `batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4`, reuses the cached merge, and sends `keep_alive=-1` on every inference request.

Verified:

- Registered weights exactly match the merged GGUF’s SHA-256.
- `ollama ps` shows the model on GPU with `UNTIL: Forever`.
- All 42 focused tests passed.

Restart the backend once to activate the startup changes. Ollama restarts will require loading the saved finetuned weights into memory again.

## User

What does that mean? Can I do it without unzipping everytime and loading the gguf as it is in the repo?

## Assistant

Yes. **You can use the merged GGUF directly without unzipping or merging the adapters again.** The finetuned weights are already saved in:

`merged_ft_adapter/batiai_gemma4_e2b_ft_merged.gguf`

Your app uses the Ollama model `batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4`, whose registered weights I verified match that file.

With the current changes:

- **Backend restart:** skips extraction, merging, copying, and registration when the GGUF and manifest exist.
- **Each chat:** reuses the loaded finetuned model.
- **Ollama or computer restart:** loads the saved finetuned weights into RAM/GPU again. That memory-loading step is necessary; it does not repeat training or merging.

Ollama serves its own registered copy of the GGUF. It doesn’t reread the repository file on every message.

## User

so if i restart my computer I don't need to finetune again?

## Assistant

Correct—**you don’t need to finetune again after restarting your computer.**

The merged finetuned weights are saved on disk. Start Ollama and your backend, and the app will load those saved weights into memory—no unzipping, training, or merging again.

Keep the merged GGUF, `merge_manifest.json`, and Ollama’s registered model files intact.

**PyTorch isn’t needed to run your finetuned GGUF through Ollama.** Ollama handles inference separately.

That warning comes from the Python `transformers` package, imported by a backend dependency. It reports that it can use tokenizers and configuration utilities but cannot run Transformers models directly without PyTorch.

Your log shows **“Application startup complete,”** so the warning did not prevent startup. The later shutdown appears orderly; this log doesn’t show what triggered it.

You don’t need to install PyTorch just to remove this warning.

## User

But is it better to have it?

## Assistant

**For your current Ollama setup, installing PyTorch won’t improve model accuracy, response speed, or GPU usage.** Ollama runs the merged GGUF independently.

PyTorch would be useful if you wanted to train adapters again or run models directly through Python’s `transformers` library.

You can leave it uninstalled for now. Your saved finetuned model will work the same.

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Zip Extraction & LoRA Adapter Weight Blending
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def ensure_zips_extracted(root_dir: Path = REPO_ROOT) -> None:
        for zip_name, expected_folder in [("coding_ft.zip", "scicompute_ft_data"), ("physchem_ft.zip", "physchem_ft_outputs")]:
            zip_path = root_dir / zip_name
            out_folder = root_dir / expected_folder
            if zip_path.exists() and not out_folder.exists():
                print(f"[FT Merger] Extracting {zip_path.name} into {root_dir}...")
                with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "r") as zf:
                    zf.extractall(root_dir)
    
    
    def locate_coding_and_physchem_adapters(root_dir: Path = REPO_ROOT) -> Tuple[Optional[Path], Optional[Path]]:
        ensure_zips_extracted(root_dir)
        coding_candidates = ["gemma_e2b_best_loss_adapter", "gemma_e2b_scicompute_dpo", "gemma_e2b_soup_candidate", "gemma_e2b_scicompute_sft"]
        physchem_candidates = ["gemma_e2b_physchem_best_adapter", "gemma_e2b_physchem_dpo", "gemma_e2b_physchem_soup_candidate", "gemma_e2b_physchem_sft"]
    
        coding_dir = None
        for name in coding_candidates:
            for match in root_dir.rglob(name):
                if (match / "adapter_model.safetensors").exists():
                    coding_dir = match
                    break
            if coding_dir:
                break
    
        physchem_dir = None
        for name in physchem_candidates:
            for match in root_dir.rglob(name):
                if (match / "adapter_model.safetensors").exists():
                    physchem_dir = match
                    break
            if physchem_dir:
                break
    
        return coding_dir, physchem_dir
    
    
    def compute_optimal_blend_alpha(root_dir: Path = REPO_ROOT, default_alpha: float = 0.50) -> float:
        try:
            c_rep_files = list(root_dir.rglob("benchmark_execution_report.json"))
            p_rep_files = list(root_dir.rglob("physchem_benchmark_report.json"))
            if c_rep_files and p_rep_files:
                c_rep = json.loads(c_rep_files[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                p_rep = json.loads(p_rep_files[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                c_loss = float(c_rep.get("best_val_loss", 1.0))
                p_loss = float(p_rep.get("best_val_loss", 1.0))
                if c_loss > 0 and p_loss > 0:
                    w_c, w_p = 1.0 / c_loss, 1.0 / p_loss
                    return round(min(max(w_c / (w_c + w_p), 0.35), 0.65), 4)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return default_alpha
    
    
    def blend_lora_adapters(
        coding_adapter_dir: Path,
        physchem_adapter_dir: Path,
        output_adapter_dir: Path = MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR,
        alpha_coding: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        if alpha_coding is None:
            alpha_coding = compute_optimal_blend_alpha(REPO_ROOT, default_alpha=0.50)
        alpha_phys = round(1.0 - alpha_coding, 4)
    
        output_adapter_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for item in coding_adapter_dir.iterdir():
            if item.is_file() and item.name != "adapter_model.safetensors":
                shutil.copy2(item, output_adapter_dir / item.name)
    
        sd_coding = load_safetensors_numpy(coding_adapter_dir / "adapter_model.safetensors")
        sd_phys = load_safetensors_numpy(physchem_adapter_dir / "adapter_model.safetensors")
    
        blended_sd = {}
        matched_tensors = 0
        for key, t_c in sd_coding.items():
            if key in sd_phys and t_c.shape == sd_phys[key].shape:
                blended_sd[key] = alpha_coding * t_c + alpha_phys * sd_phys[key]
                matched_tensors += 1
            else:
                blended_sd[key] = t_c
    
        save_safetensors_fp16(blended_sd, output_adapter_dir / "adapter_model.safetensors")
        manifest = {
            "coding_adapter": str(coding_adapter_dir),
            "physchem_adapter": str(physchem_adapter_dir),
            "output_adapter": str(output_adapter_dir),
            "alpha_coding": alpha_coding,
            "alpha_physchem": alpha_phys,
            "matched_lora_tensors": matched_tensors,
            "total_tensors": len(blended_sd),
        }
        (output_adapter_dir / "merge_manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"[FT Merger] Blended {matched_tensors}/{len(blended_sd)} LoRA tensors ({alpha_coding*100:.1f}% Coding + {alpha_phys*100:.1f}% PhysChem) -> {output_adapter_dir}")
        return manifest
    
    
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. GGUF Binary Header Parser & Block Dequantizer/Requantizer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _read_gguf_str(f) -> str:
    
    
    def patch_ollama_gguf_with_blended_lora(
        base_gguf_path: Path,
        adapter_dir: Path = MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR,
        out_gguf_path: Path = PATCHED_GGUF_PATH,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        adapter_cfg = json.loads((adapter_dir / "adapter_config.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        r = float(adapter_cfg.get("r", 32))
        lora_alpha = float(adapter_cfg.get("lora_alpha", 64))
        use_rslora = bool(adapter_cfg.get("use_rslora", True))
        scale = (lora_alpha / math.sqrt(r)) if use_rslora else (lora_alpha / r)
    
        lora_tensors = load_safetensors_numpy(adapter_dir / "adapter_model.safetensors")
        pat = re.compile(r"layers\.(\d+)\.(self_attn\.(?:q_proj|k_proj|v_proj|o_proj)|mlp\.(?:gate_proj|up_proj|down_proj))\.(lora_[AB])\.weight$")
    
        pairs: Dict[Tuple[str, str], Dict[str, np.ndarray]] = {}
        for key, arr in lora_tensors.items():
            m = pat.search(key)
            if not m:
                continue
            layer_idx, hf_proj, ab_tag = m.groups()
            pairs.setdefault((layer_idx, hf_proj), {})[ab_tag] = arr
    
        print(f"[GGUF Patcher] Copying base `batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4` GGUF blob ({base_gguf_path.stat().st_size / (1024**3):.2f} GiB) -> {out_gguf_path.name}...")
        shutil.copy2(base_gguf_path, out_gguf_path)
    
        data_offset, gguf_tensors = parse_gguf_tensors(out_gguf_path)
        patched_layers = 0
        skipped_layers = 0
    
        with open(out_gguf_path, "r+b") as f:
            for (layer_idx, hf_proj), ab_dict in sorted(pairs.items(), key=lambda x: (int(x[0][0]), x[0][1])):
                if "lora_A" not in ab_dict or "lora_B" not in ab_dict:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                gguf_suf = HF_TO_GGUF_SUFFIX[hf_proj]
                cand_names = [
                    f"blk.{layer_idx}.{gguf_suf}",
                    f"language_model.blk.{layer_idx}.{gguf_suf}",
                    f"model.layers.{layer_idx}.{hf_proj}.weight",
                ]
                gguf_name = next((c for c in cand_names if c in gguf_tensors), None)
                if gguf_name is None:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                t_info = gguf_tensors[gguf_name]
                ggml_type = t_info["ggml_type"]
                dims = t_info["dims"]
                if ggml_type not in GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES or len(dims) != 2:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                in_feat, out_feat = int(dims[0]), int(dims[1])
                lora_A, lora_B = ab_dict["lora_A"], ab_dict["lora_B"]
                if lora_A.shape[1] != in_feat or lora_B.shape[0] != out_feat:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                delta_w = scale * (lora_B @ lora_A)
                block_elems, block_bytes = GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES[ggml_type]
                n_bytes = ((in_feat * out_feat) // block_elems) * block_bytes
                abs_offset = data_offset + int(t_info["rel_offset"])
    
                f.seek(abs_offset)
                orig_bytes = f.read(n_bytes)
                patched_bytes = patch_gguf_tensor_bytes(orig_bytes, ggml_type, delta_w)
                f.seek(abs_offset)
                f.write(patched_bytes)
                patched_layers += 1
    
        print(f"[GGUF Patcher] Patched {patched_layers}/{len(pairs)} LoRA matrix pairs directly into {out_gguf_path.name}!")
        return {"patched_matrices": patched_layers, "skipped_matrices": skipped_layers, "gguf_path": str(out_gguf_path)}
    
    
    def build_patched_modelfile(orig_modelfile: str, orig_blob_path: Path, patched_gguf_path: Path) -> Path:
        out_lines = []
        replaced_main_from = False
        for line in orig_modelfile.splitlines():
            s = line.strip()
            if s.startswith("#"):
                continue
            if s.upper().startswith("FROM ") and not replaced_main_from and str(orig_blob_path) in s:
                out_lines.append(f"FROM {patched_gguf_path.resolve()}")
                replaced_main_from = True
            elif s.upper().startswith("SYSTEM "):
                continue
            else:
                out_lines.append(line)
    
        if not replaced_main_from:
            out_lines.insert(0, f"FROM {patched_gguf_path.resolve()}")
    
        out_lines.append("PARAMETER temperature 0.1")
        out_lines.append("PARAMETER top_p 0.90")
        out_lines.append("PARAMETER num_ctx 8192")
        out_lines.append(f'SYSTEM """{UNIFIED_SYSTEM_PROMPT}"""')
    
        modelfile_patched = MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "Modelfile.batiai_patched"
        modelfile_patched.write_text("\n".join(out_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return modelfile_patched
    
    
    def register_unified_model_in_ollama(
        target_model_name: str = TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        base_ollama_model: str = TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        alpha_coding: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        # Fast-skip if the patched GGUF and merge manifest already exist.
        if PATCHED_GGUF_PATH.exists() and (MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "merge_manifest.json").exists():
            manifest = json.loads((MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "merge_manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            return {"status": "success", "mode": "cached_in_place_gguf", "active_model": target_model_name, "manifest": manifest}
    
        coding_dir, physchem_dir = locate_coding_and_physchem_adapters(REPO_ROOT)
        if not coding_dir or not physchem_dir:
            return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "coding_ft.zip or physchem_ft.zip adapters not found."}
    
        manifest = blend_lora_adapters(coding_dir, physchem_dir, MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR, alpha_coding=alpha_coding)
        base_blob, orig_modelfile = find_ollama_gguf_blob_and_modelfile(base_ollama_model)
    
        if base_blob is not None:
            patch_stats = patch_ollama_gguf_with_blended_lora(base_blob, MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR, PATCHED_GGUF_PATH)
            manifest.update(patch_stats)
            modelfile_path = build_patched_modelfile(orig_modelfile, base_blob, PATCHED_GGUF_PATH)
    
            res = subprocess.run(["ollama", "create", target_model_name, "-f", str(modelfile_path)], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", check=False)
            if res.returncode == 0:
                print(f"[FT Merger] Successfully updated `{target_model_name}` in Ollama with in-place LoRA-patched GGUF weights!")
                return {
                    "status": "success",
                    "mode": "in_place_gguf_lora_merged",
                    "active_model": target_model_name,
                    "manifest": manifest,
                }
            return {"status": "error", "error": res.stderr.strip(), "manifest": manifest}
    
        return {"status": "merged_adapter_only", "active_model": target_model_name, "manifest": manifest}
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        print(json.dumps(register_unified_model_in_ollama(), indent=2))

            layer_idx, hf_proj, ab_tag = m.groups()
            pairs.setdefault((layer_idx, hf_proj), {})[ab_tag] = arr
    
        print(f"[GGUF Patcher] Copying base `batiai/gemma4-e2b:q4` GGUF blob ({base_gguf_path.stat().st_size / (1024**3):.2f} GiB) -> {out_gguf_path.name}...")
        shutil.copy2(base_gguf_path, out_gguf_path)
    
        data_offset, gguf_tensors = parse_gguf_tensors(out_gguf_path)
        patched_layers = 0
        skipped_layers = 0
    
        with open(out_gguf_path, "r+b") as f:
            for (layer_idx, hf_proj), ab_dict in sorted(pairs.items(), key=lambda x: (int(x[0][0]), x[0][1])):
                if "lora_A" not in ab_dict or "lora_B" not in ab_dict:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                gguf_suf = HF_TO_GGUF_SUFFIX[hf_proj]
                cand_names = [
                    f"blk.{layer_idx}.{gguf_suf}",
                    f"language_model.blk.{layer_idx}.{gguf_suf}",
                    f"model.layers.{layer_idx}.{hf_proj}.weight",
                ]
                gguf_name = next((c for c in cand_names if c in gguf_tensors), None)
                if gguf_name is None:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                t_info = gguf_tensors[gguf_name]
                ggml_type = t_info["ggml_type"]
                dims = t_info["dims"]
                if ggml_type not in GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES or len(dims) != 2:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                in_feat, out_feat = int(dims[0]), int(dims[1])
                lora_A, lora_B = ab_dict["lora_A"], ab_dict["lora_B"]
                if lora_A.shape[1] != in_feat or lora_B.shape[0] != out_feat:
                    skipped_layers += 1
                    continue
    
                delta_w = scale * (lora_B @ lora_A)
                block_elems, block_bytes = GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES[ggml_type]
                n_bytes = ((in_feat * out_feat) // block_elems) * block_bytes
                abs_offset = data_offset + int(t_info["rel_offset"])
    
                f.seek(abs_offset)
                orig_bytes = f.read(n_bytes)
                patched_bytes = patch_gguf_tensor_bytes(orig_bytes, ggml_type, delta_w)
                f.seek(abs_offset)
                f.write(patched_bytes)
                patched_layers += 1
    
        print(f"[GGUF Patcher] Patched {patched_layers}/{len(pairs)} LoRA matrix pairs directly into {out_gguf_path.name}!")
        return {"patched_matrices": patched_layers, "skipped_matrices": skipped_layers, "gguf_path": str(out_gguf_path)}
    
    
    def build_patched_modelfile(orig_modelfile: str, orig_blob_path: Path, patched_gguf_path: Path) -> Path:
        out_lines = []
        replaced_main_from = False
        for line in orig_modelfile.splitlines():
            s = line.strip()
            if s.startswith("#"):
                continue
            if s.upper().startswith("FROM ") and not replaced_main_from and str(orig_blob_path) in s:
                out_lines.append(f"FROM {patched_gguf_path.resolve()}")
                replaced_main_from = True
            elif s.upper().startswith("SYSTEM "):
                continue
            else:
                out_lines.append(line)
    
        if not replaced_main_from:
            out_lines.insert(0, f"FROM {patched_gguf_path.resolve()}")
    
        out_lines.append("PARAMETER temperature 0.1")
        out_lines.append("PARAMETER top_p 0.90")
        out_lines.append("PARAMETER num_ctx 8192")
        out_lines.append(f'SYSTEM """{UNIFIED_SYSTEM_PROMPT}"""')
    
        modelfile_patched = MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "Modelfile.batiai_patched"
        modelfile_patched.write_text("\n".join(out_lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return modelfile_patched
    
    
    def register_unified_model_in_ollama(
        target_model_name: str = TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        base_ollama_model: str = TARGET_OLLAMA_MODEL,
        alpha_coding: Optional[float] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        # Fast-skip if the patched GGUF and merge manifest already exist.
        if PATCHED_GGUF_PATH.exists() and (MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "merge_manifest.json").exists():
            manifest = json.loads((MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR / "merge_manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            return {"status": "success", "mode": "cached_in_place_gguf", "active_model": target_model_name, "manifest": manifest}
    
        coding_dir, physchem_dir = locate_coding_and_physchem_adapters(REPO_ROOT)
        if not coding_dir or not physchem_dir:
            return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "coding_ft.zip or physchem_ft.zip adapters not found."}
    
        manifest = blend_lora_adapters(coding_dir, physchem_dir, MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR, alpha_coding=alpha_coding)
        base_blob, orig_modelfile = find_ollama_gguf_blob_and_modelfile(base_ollama_model)
    
        if base_blob is not None:
            patch_stats = patch_ollama_gguf_with_blended_lora(base_blob, MERGED_ADAPTER_DIR, PATCHED_GGUF_PATH)
            manifest.update(patch_stats)
            modelfile_path = build_patched_modelfile(orig_modelfile, base_blob, PATCHED_GGUF_PATH)
    
            res = subprocess.run(["ollama", "create", target_model_name, "-f", str(modelfile_path)], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", check=False)
            if res.returncode == 0:
                print(f"[FT Merger] Successfully updated `{target_model_name}` in Ollama with in-place LoRA-patched GGUF weights!")
                return {
                    "status": "success",
                    "mode": "in_place_gguf_lora_merged",
                    "active_model": target_model_name,
                    "manifest": manifest,
                }
            return {"status": "error", "error": res.stderr.strip(), "manifest": manifest}
    
        return {"status": "merged_adapter_only", "active_model": target_model_name, "manifest": manifest}
    
    
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        print(json.dumps(register_unified_model_in_ollama(), indent=2))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _read_gguf_str(f) -> str:
    
    
    def patch_gguf_tensor_bytes(raw_bytes: bytes, ggml_type: int, delta_w: np.ndarray) -> bytes:
        """Dequantizes a GGUF tensor byte slice, adds `delta_w`, and re-quantizes to the exact same format."""
        flat_delta = delta_w.reshape(-1)
        if ggml_type == 0:  # F32
            w = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype="<f4").copy() + flat_delta
            return w.astype("<f4").tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 1:  # F16
            w = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype="<f2").astype(np.float32) + flat_delta
            return w.astype("<f2").tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 30:  # BF16
            u16 = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype="<u2")
            w = (u16.astype(np.uint32) << 16).view(np.float32) + flat_delta
            new_u16 = (w.view(np.uint32) >> 16).astype("<u2")
            return new_u16.tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 2:  # Q4_0
            n_blocks = len(raw_bytes) // 18
            dt = np.dtype([("d", "<f2"), ("qs", "u1", (16,))])
            blocks = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=dt).copy()
            d = blocks["d"].astype(np.float32)[:, None]
            qs = blocks["qs"]
            lo = (qs & 0x0F).astype(np.float32) - 8.0
            hi = (qs >> 4).astype(np.float32) - 8.0
            w_new = np.concatenate([lo, hi], axis=1) * d + flat_delta.reshape(n_blocks, 32)
            d_new = (np.max(np.abs(w_new), axis=1, keepdims=True) / 8.0).astype(np.float16)
            d_f = d_new.astype(np.float32)
            q = np.where(d_f > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(w_new / np.maximum(d_f, 1e-12)) + 8.0, 0, 15), 8).astype(np.uint8)
            blocks["d"] = d_new.squeeze(1)
            blocks["qs"] = (q[:, :16] & 0x0F) | ((q[:, 16:] & 0x0F) << 4)
            return blocks.tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 3:  # Q4_1
            n_blocks = len(raw_bytes) // 20
            dt = np.dtype([("d", "<f2"), ("m", "<f2"), ("qs", "u1", (16,))])
            blocks = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=dt).copy()
            d = blocks["d"].astype(np.float32)[:, None]
            m = blocks["m"].astype(np.float32)[:, None]
            qs = blocks["qs"]
            w_new = np.concatenate([(qs & 0x0F).astype(np.float32) * d + m, (qs >> 4).astype(np.float32) * d + m], axis=1) + flat_delta.reshape(n_blocks, 32)
            w_min = np.min(w_new, axis=1, keepdims=True)
            d_new = ((np.max(w_new, axis=1, keepdims=True) - w_min) / 15.0).astype(np.float16)
            m_new = w_min.astype(np.float16)
            d_f, m_f = d_new.astype(np.float32), m_new.astype(np.float32)
            q = np.where(d_f > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round((w_new - m_f) / np.maximum(d_f, 1e-12)), 0, 15), 0).astype(np.uint8)
            blocks["d"] = d_new.squeeze(1)
            blocks["m"] = m_new.squeeze(1)
            blocks["qs"] = (q[:, :16] & 0x0F) | ((q[:, 16:] & 0x0F) << 4)
            return blocks.tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 8:  # Q8_0
            n_blocks = len(raw_bytes) // 34
            dt = np.dtype([("d", "<f2"), ("qs", "i1", (32,))])
            blocks = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=dt).copy()
            d = blocks["d"].astype(np.float32)[:, None]
            w_new = blocks["qs"].astype(np.float32) * d + flat_delta.reshape(n_blocks, 32)
            d_new = (np.max(np.abs(w_new), axis=1, keepdims=True) / 127.0).astype(np.float16)
            d_f = d_new.astype(np.float32)
            blocks["d"] = d_new.squeeze(1)
            blocks["qs"] = np.where(d_f > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(w_new / np.maximum(d_f, 1e-12)), -127, 127), 0).astype(np.int8)
            return blocks.tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 12:  # Q4_K
            n_blocks = len(raw_bytes) // 144
            dt = np.dtype([("d", "<f2"), ("dmin", "<f2"), ("scales", "u1", (12,)), ("qs", "u1", (128,))])
            blocks = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=dt).copy()
            d = blocks["d"].astype(np.float32)
            dmin = blocks["dmin"].astype(np.float32)
            sc_raw, qs = blocks["scales"], blocks["qs"]
            sc = np.zeros((n_blocks, 8), dtype=np.float32)
            mn = np.zeros((n_blocks, 8), dtype=np.float32)
            for j in range(4):
                sc[:, j] = sc_raw[:, j] & 63
                mn[:, j] = sc_raw[:, j + 4] & 63
            for j in range(4, 8):
                sc[:, j] = (sc_raw[:, j + 4] & 0x0F) | ((sc_raw[:, j - 4] >> 6) << 4)
                mn[:, j] = (sc_raw[:, j + 4] >> 4) | ((sc_raw[:, j] >> 6) << 4)
            w = np.zeros((n_blocks, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            for chunk in range(4):
                q_c = qs[:, chunk * 32 : (chunk + 1) * 32]
                w[:, chunk * 64 : chunk * 64 + 32] = (q_c & 0x0F).astype(np.float32) * (d * sc[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None] - (dmin * mn[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None]
                w[:, chunk * 64 + 32 : (chunk + 1) * 64] = (q_c >> 4).astype(np.float32) * (d * sc[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None] - (dmin * mn[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None]
            w_new = w + flat_delta.reshape(n_blocks, 256)
            w_sub = w_new.reshape(n_blocks, 8, 32)
            m_sub = np.maximum(0.0, -np.min(w_sub, axis=2))
            d_sub = np.maximum(0.0, (np.max(w_sub, axis=2) + m_sub) / 15.0)
            d_super = (np.max(d_sub, axis=1) / 63.0).astype(np.float16)
            dmin_super = (np.max(m_sub, axis=1) / 63.0).astype(np.float16)
            d_sup_f, dm_sup_f = d_super.astype(np.float32), dmin_super.astype(np.float32)
            sc_q = np.where(d_sup_f[:, None] > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(d_sub / np.maximum(d_sup_f[:, None], 1e-12)), 0, 63), 0).astype(np.uint8)
            mn_q = np.where(dm_sup_f[:, None] > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(m_sub / np.maximum(dm_sup_f[:, None], 1e-12)), 0, 63), 0).astype(np.uint8)
            new_sc_raw = np.zeros((n_blocks, 12), dtype=np.uint8)
            for j in range(4):
                new_sc_raw[:, j] = (sc_q[:, j] & 63) | ((sc_q[:, j + 4] >> 4) << 6)
                new_sc_raw[:, j + 4] = (mn_q[:, j] & 63) | ((mn_q[:, j + 4] >> 4) << 6)
                new_sc_raw[:, j + 8] = (sc_q[:, j + 4] & 0x0F) | ((mn_q[:, j + 4] & 0x0F) << 4)
            new_qs = np.zeros((n_blocks, 128), dtype=np.uint8)
            for chunk in range(4):
                d1, m1 = (d_sup_f * sc_q[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None], (dm_sup_f * mn_q[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None]
                d2, m2 = (d_sup_f * sc_q[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None], (dm_sup_f * mn_q[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None]
                w1, w2 = w_new[:, chunk * 64 : chunk * 64 + 32], w_new[:, chunk * 64 + 32 : (chunk + 1) * 64]
                q1 = np.where(d1 > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round((w1 + m1) / np.maximum(d1, 1e-12)), 0, 15), 0).astype(np.uint8)
                q2 = np.where(d2 > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round((w2 + m2) / np.maximum(d2, 1e-12)), 0, 15), 0).astype(np.uint8)
                new_qs[:, chunk * 32 : (chunk + 1) * 32] = (q1 & 0x0F) | ((q2 & 0x0F) << 4)
            blocks["d"], blocks["dmin"], blocks["scales"], blocks["qs"] = d_super, dmin_super, new_sc_raw, new_qs
            return blocks.tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 13:  # Q5_K
            n_blocks = len(raw_bytes) // 176
            dt = np.dtype([("d", "<f2"), ("dmin", "<f2"), ("scales", "u1", (12,)), ("qh", "u1", (32,)), ("qs", "u1", (128,))])
            blocks = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=dt).copy()
            d, dmin = blocks["d"].astype(np.float32), blocks["dmin"].astype(np.float32)
            sc_raw, qh, qs = blocks["scales"], blocks["qh"], blocks["qs"]
            sc = np.zeros((n_blocks, 8), dtype=np.float32)
            mn = np.zeros((n_blocks, 8), dtype=np.float32)
            for j in range(4):
                sc[:, j], mn[:, j] = sc_raw[:, j] & 63, sc_raw[:, j + 4] & 63
            for j in range(4, 8):
                sc[:, j] = (sc_raw[:, j + 4] & 0x0F) | ((sc_raw[:, j - 4] >> 6) << 4)
                mn[:, j] = (sc_raw[:, j + 4] >> 4) | ((sc_raw[:, j] >> 6) << 4)
            w = np.zeros((n_blocks, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            for chunk in range(4):
                q_c = qs[:, chunk * 32 : (chunk + 1) * 32]
                h1, h2 = ((qh >> (2 * chunk)) & 1) << 4, ((qh >> (2 * chunk + 1)) & 1) << 4
                w[:, chunk * 64 : chunk * 64 + 32] = ((q_c & 0x0F) | h1).astype(np.float32) * (d * sc[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None] - (dmin * mn[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None]
                w[:, chunk * 64 + 32 : (chunk + 1) * 64] = ((q_c >> 4) | h2).astype(np.float32) * (d * sc[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None] - (dmin * mn[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None]
            w_new = w + flat_delta.reshape(n_blocks, 256)
            w_sub = w_new.reshape(n_blocks, 8, 32)
            m_sub = np.maximum(0.0, -np.min(w_sub, axis=2))
            d_sub = np.maximum(0.0, (np.max(w_sub, axis=2) + m_sub) / 31.0)
            d_super, dmin_super = (np.max(d_sub, axis=1) / 63.0).astype(np.float16), (np.max(m_sub, axis=1) / 63.0).astype(np.float16)
            d_sup_f, dm_sup_f = d_super.astype(np.float32), dmin_super.astype(np.float32)
            sc_q = np.where(d_sup_f[:, None] > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(d_sub / np.maximum(d_sup_f[:, None], 1e-12)), 0, 63), 0).astype(np.uint8)
            mn_q = np.where(dm_sup_f[:, None] > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(m_sub / np.maximum(dm_sup_f[:, None], 1e-12)), 0, 63), 0).astype(np.uint8)
            new_sc_raw = np.zeros((n_blocks, 12), dtype=np.uint8)
            for j in range(4):
                new_sc_raw[:, j] = (sc_q[:, j] & 63) | ((sc_q[:, j + 4] >> 4) << 6)
                new_sc_raw[:, j + 4] = (mn_q[:, j] & 63) | ((mn_q[:, j + 4] >> 4) << 6)
                new_sc_raw[:, j + 8] = (sc_q[:, j + 4] & 0x0F) | ((mn_q[:, j + 4] & 0x0F) << 4)
            new_qs = np.zeros((n_blocks, 128), dtype=np.uint8)
            new_qh = np.zeros((n_blocks, 32), dtype=np.uint8)
            for chunk in range(4):
                d1, m1 = (d_sup_f * sc_q[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None], (dm_sup_f * mn_q[:, 2 * chunk])[:, None]
                d2, m2 = (d_sup_f * sc_q[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None], (dm_sup_f * mn_q[:, 2 * chunk + 1])[:, None]
                w1, w2 = w_new[:, chunk * 64 : chunk * 64 + 32], w_new[:, chunk * 64 + 32 : (chunk + 1) * 64]
                q1 = np.where(d1 > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round((w1 + m1) / np.maximum(d1, 1e-12)), 0, 31), 0).astype(np.uint8)
                q2 = np.where(d2 > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round((w2 + m2) / np.maximum(d2, 1e-12)), 0, 31), 0).astype(np.uint8)
                new_qs[:, chunk * 32 : (chunk + 1) * 32] = (q1 & 0x0F) | ((q2 & 0x0F) << 4)
                new_qh |= (((q1 >> 4) & 1) << (2 * chunk)) | (((q2 >> 4) & 1) << (2 * chunk + 1))
            blocks["d"], blocks["dmin"], blocks["scales"], blocks["qh"], blocks["qs"] = d_super, dmin_super, new_sc_raw, new_qh, new_qs
            return blocks.tobytes()
        if ggml_type == 14:  # Q6_K
            n_blocks = len(raw_bytes) // 210
            dt = np.dtype([("ql", "u1", (128,)), ("qh", "u1", (64,)), ("scales", "i1", (16,)), ("d", "<f2")])
            blocks = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, dtype=dt).copy()
            d, sc, ql, qh = blocks["d"].astype(np.float32), blocks["scales"].astype(np.float32), blocks["ql"], blocks["qh"]
            w = np.zeros((n_blocks, 256), dtype=np.float32)
            for half in range(2):
                ql_h, qh_h, sc_h = ql[:, half * 64 : (half + 1) * 64], qh[:, half * 32 : (half + 1) * 32], sc[:, half * 8 : (half + 1) * 8]
                q1 = ((ql_h[:, 0:32] & 0x0F) | (((qh_h >> 0) & 3) << 4)).astype(np.float32) - 32.0
                q2 = ((ql_h[:, 32:64] & 0x0F) | (((qh_h >> 2) & 3) << 4)).astype(np.float32) - 32.0
                q3 = ((ql_h[:, 0:32] >> 4) | (((qh_h >> 4) & 3) << 4)).astype(np.float32) - 32.0
                q4 = ((ql_h[:, 32:64] >> 4) | (((qh_h >> 6) & 3) << 4)).astype(np.float32) - 32.0
                for sub_idx, q_arr in enumerate((q1, q2, q3, q4)):
                    base = half * 128 + sub_idx * 32
                    w[:, base : base + 16] = d[:, None] * sc_h[:, 2 * sub_idx, None] * q_arr[:, :16]
                    w[:, base + 16 : base + 32] = d[:, None] * sc_h[:, 2 * sub_idx + 1, None] * q_arr[:, 16:]
            w_new = w + flat_delta.reshape(n_blocks, 256)
            w_sub = w_new.reshape(n_blocks, 16, 16)
            d_sub = np.max(np.abs(w_sub), axis=2) / 31.0
            d_super = (np.max(d_sub, axis=1) / 127.0).astype(np.float16)
            d_sup_f = d_super.astype(np.float32)
            sc_q = np.where(d_sup_f[:, None] > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(d_sub / np.maximum(d_sup_f[:, None], 1e-12)), -127, 127), 0).astype(np.int8)
            eff_scale = d_sup_f[:, None, None] * sc_q.astype(np.float32)[:, :, None]
            q_flat = np.where(np.abs(eff_scale) > 1e-12, np.clip(np.round(w_sub / np.where(np.abs(eff_scale) > 1e-12, eff_scale, 1.0)) + 32.0, 0, 63), 32).astype(np.uint8).reshape(n_blocks, 256)
            new_ql, new_qh = np.zeros((n_blocks, 128), dtype=np.uint8), np.zeros((n_blocks, 64), dtype=np.uint8)
            for half in range(2):
                b = half * 128
                q1, q2, q3, q4 = q_flat[:, b : b + 32], q_flat[:, b + 32 : b + 64], q_flat[:, b + 64 : b + 96], q_flat[:, b + 96 : b + 128]

                new_ql[:, half * 64 + 32 : (half + 1) * 64] = (q2 & 0x0F) | ((q4 & 0x0F) << 4)

            blocks["ql"], blocks["qh"], blocks["scales"], blocks["d"] = new_ql, new_qh, sc_q, d_super
            return blocks.tobytes()

    # 1. Pure-NumPy SafeTensors Reader & Writer
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def load_safetensors_numpy(st_path: Path) -> Dict[str, np.ndarray]:
        with open(st_path, "rb") as f:
            header_len = struct.unpack("<Q", f.read(8))[0]
            header = json.loads(f.read(header_len).decode("utf-8"))
            data_start = 8 + header_len
            tensors = {}
            for name, meta in header.items():
                if name == "__metadata__":
                    continue
                dtype_str = meta["dtype"]
                shape = tuple(meta["shape"])
                start, end = meta["data_offsets"]
                f.seek(data_start + start)
                raw = f.read(end - start)
                if dtype_str == "F16":
                    arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype="<f2").astype(np.float32).reshape(shape)
                elif dtype_str == "BF16":
                    u16 = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype="<u2")
                    arr = (u16.astype(np.uint32) << 16).view(np.float32).reshape(shape)
                elif dtype_str == "F32":
                    arr = np.frombuffer(raw, dtype="<f4").copy().reshape(shape)
                else:
                    continue
                tensors[name] = arr
        return tensors
    
    
    def save_safetensors_fp16(tensors: Dict[str, np.ndarray], st_path: Path) -> None:
        header = {}
        offset = 0
        raw_chunks = []
        for name, arr in tensors.items():
            arr16 = arr.astype("<f2")
            b = arr16.tobytes()
            header[name] = {
                "dtype": "F16",
                "shape": list(arr16.shape),
                "data_offsets": [offset, offset + len(b)],
            }
            raw_chunks.append(b)
            offset += len(b)
        header_bytes = json.dumps(header, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
        pad = (8 - (len(header_bytes) % 8)) % 8
        header_bytes += b" " * pad
        with open(st_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(struct.pack("<Q", len(header_bytes)))
            f.write(header_bytes)
            for chunk in raw_chunks:
                f.write(chunk)
    
    
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Zip Extraction & LoRA Adapter Weight Blending
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def ensure_zips_extracted(root_dir: Path = REPO_ROOT) -> None:
        for zip_name, expected_folder in [("coding_ft.zip", "scicompute_ft_data"), ("physchem_ft.zip", "physchem_ft_outputs")]:
        n = struct.unpack("<Q", f.read(8))[0]
        return f.read(n).decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    
    
    def _skip_gguf_val(f, vtype: int) -> None:
        sizes = {0: 1, 1: 1, 2: 2, 3: 2, 4: 4, 5: 4, 6: 4, 7: 1, 10: 8, 11: 8, 12: 8}
        if vtype in sizes:
            f.seek(sizes[vtype], 1)
        elif vtype == 8:
            n = struct.unpack("<Q", f.read(8))[0]
            f.seek(n, 1)
        elif vtype == 9:
            elem_type, arr_len = struct.unpack("<IQ", f.read(12))
            if elem_type in sizes:
                f.seek(sizes[elem_type] * arr_len, 1)
            else:
                for _ in range(arr_len):
                    _skip_gguf_val(f, elem_type)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported GGUF metadata type {vtype}")
    
    
    def parse_gguf_tensors(gguf_path: Path) -> Tuple[int, Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]:
        with open(gguf_path, "rb") as f:
            if f.read(4) != b"GGUF":
                raise ValueError(f"{gguf_path} is not a valid GGUF file")
            version, n_tensors, n_kv = struct.unpack("<IQQ", f.read(20))
            alignment = 32
            for _ in range(n_kv):
                key = _read_gguf_str(f)
                vtype = struct.unpack("<I", f.read(4))[0]
                if key == "general.alignment" and vtype == 4:
                    alignment = struct.unpack("<I", f.read(4))[0]
                else:
                    _skip_gguf_val(f, vtype)
            tensors = {}
            for _ in range(n_tensors):
                t_name = _read_gguf_str(f)
                n_dims = struct.unpack("<I", f.read(4))[0]
                dims = [struct.unpack("<Q", f.read(8))[0] for _ in range(n_dims)]
                ggml_type, rel_offset = struct.unpack("<IQ", f.read(12))
                tensors[t_name] = {"dims": dims, "ggml_type": ggml_type, "rel_offset": rel_offset}
            pos = f.tell()
            data_offset = (pos + alignment - 1) // alignment * alignment
        return data_offset, tensors

    from backend.sandbox.executor import SandboxExecutor
    from typing import Dict, Optional
    
    def run_python_in_sandbox(code: str, data_mount_path: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict:
        """Executes python code inside the Docker Sandbox and returns stdout/stderr/artifacts"""
        executor = SandboxExecutor()
        print(f"Executing Python in Sandbox... Data mount: {data_mount_path}")
        return executor.run_python_script(code, data_mount_path)
    
    def run_bash_in_sandbox(script: str, data_mount_path: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict:
        """Executes a bash script inside the Docker Sandbox"""
        executor = SandboxExecutor()
        # To run bash, we can wrap the python executor or add a specific bash method to it
        # Here we simulate it by wrapping the bash command in a python subprocess for simplicity,
        # or ideally add run_bash_script to the SandboxExecutor.
        python_wrapper = f'''import subprocess
    import sys
    script = r"""{script}"""
    with open('script.sh', 'w') as f:
        f.write(script)
    result = subprocess.run(['bash', 'script.sh'], capture_output=True, text=True)
    sys.stdout.write(result.stdout)
    sys.stderr.write(result.stderr)
    sys.exit(result.returncode)
    '''
        print("Executing Bash in Sandbox...")
        return executor.run_python_script(python_wrapper, data_mount_path)
