    import subprocess
    import tempfile
    from typing import Dict, Optional, List
    
    try:
        import docker
    except ImportError:
        docker = None
    
    def _win_to_wsl_path(path: str) -> str:
        r"""Converts a Windows path (e.g. C:\Users\...) to a WSL mount path (/mnt/c/Users/...)."""
        abs_path = os.path.abspath(path)
        drive, rest = os.path.splitdrive(abs_path)
        if drive:
            drive_letter = drive.replace(":", "").lower()
            rest_forward = rest.replace("\\", "/")
            return f"/mnt/{drive_letter}{rest_forward}"
        return path.replace("\\", "/")
    
    class SandboxExecutor:
        def __init__(self, image_name: str = "chatbox-sandbox:latest"):
            self.image_name = image_name
            self.client = None
            self.wsl_available = False
    
            # Attempt 1: Native docker Python client (Docker Desktop named pipe / socket)
            if docker:
                try:
                    self.client = docker.from_env()
                    self.client.ping()
                except Exception:
                    self.client = None
    
            # Attempt 2: WSL2 Docker fallback if native client is unavailable
            if not self.client and shutil.which("wsl"):
                try:
                    check = subprocess.run(
                        ["wsl", "-d", "Ubuntu", "docker", "images", "-q", self.image_name],
                        capture_output=True,
                        text=True,
                        timeout=10
                    )
                    if check.returncode == 0:
                        self.wsl_available = True
                except Exception:
                    self.wsl_available = False
    
            self._ensure_image()
    
        def _ensure_image(self):
            if self.client:
                try:
                    self.client.images.get(self.image_name)
                except Exception:
                    print(f"Building Docker image {self.image_name}...")
                    dockerfile_path = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
                    self.client.images.build(path=dockerfile_path, tag=self.image_name)
                    print("Build complete.")
    
        def run_python_script(self, script_content: str, data_file_path: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict:
            """
            Runs a Python script in an isolated, secure Docker sandbox container.
            Enforces strict containment guarantees:
            - network_mode='none' (zero network access, completely isolated)
            - mem_limit='2g' (2 GB maximum RAM cap)
            - pids_limit=50 (prevent fork bombs)
            - read_only=True (root filesystem is mounted strictly read-only)
            - MPLCONFIGDIR and TMPDIR directed to writable /tmp/workspace mount
            - Data files mounted strictly read-only at /mnt/data/<basename>
            - Container auto-removes on completion
            """
            if not self.client and not self.wsl_available:
                return {
                    "stdout": "",
                    "stderr": "Docker is not running on the host (neither Docker Desktop nor WSL Docker daemon reachable).",
                    "exit_code": 1,
                    "artifacts": []
                }
    
            # Create temporary workspace directory on host
            workspace_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="chatbox_workspace_")
            try:
                script_path = os.path.join(workspace_dir, "script.py")

        from pathlib import Path
        p=Path('backend/tests/test_lora_blend.py')
        p.write_text('''import json
        import math
        import numpy as np
        import pytest
        from backend.merge_finetuned_models import blend_lora_adapters, save_safetensors_fp16, load_safetensors_numpy
        
        @pytest.mark.parametrize("rslora", [True, False])
        def test_blend_preserves_weighted_updates(tmp_path, rslora):
            dirs = [tmp_path / name for name in ("coding", "phys", "merged")]
            akey = "model.layers.0.self_attn.q_proj.lora_A.weight"
            bkey = akey.replace("lora_A", "lora_B")
            ac = np.array([[1., 2.], [3., 4.]])
            bc = np.array([[2., 1.], [0., 3.]])
            ap = np.array([[4., 0.], [1., 2.]])
            bp = np.array([[1., 3.], [2., 0.]])
            for folder, a, b in zip(dirs[:2], (ac, ap), (bc, bp)):
                folder.mkdir()
                (folder / "adapter_config.json").write_text(json.dumps({"r": 2, "lora_alpha": 4, "use_rslora": rslora, "base_model_name_or_path": "same-base"}))
                save_safetensors_fp16({akey: a, bkey: b}, folder / "adapter_model.safetensors")
            blend_lora_adapters(*dirs, alpha_coding=.35)
            cfg = json.loads((dirs[2] / "adapter_config.json").read_text())
            merged = load_safetensors_numpy(dirs[2] / "adapter_model.safetensors")
            scale = cfg["lora_alpha"] / (math.sqrt(cfg["r"]) if rslora else cfg["r"])
            original_scale = 4 / (math.sqrt(2) if rslora else 2)
            np.testing.assert_allclose(scale * (merged[bkey] @ merged[akey]), original_scale * (.35 * bc @ ac + .65 * bp @ ap), rtol=.001)

    $ @'
        from pathlib import Path
        p=Path('backend/rebuild_finetuned_gguf.py')
        p.write_text('''"""Rebuild from the original GGUF, retaining merged matrices in F16."""
        import json
        import math
        import struct
        from pathlib import Path
        import numpy as np
        from backend import merge_finetuned_models as m
        
        
        def rebuild(base: Path, adapter: Path, destination: Path):
            offset, tensors = m.parse_gguf_tensors(base)
            cfg = json.loads((adapter / "adapter_config.json").read_text())
            scale = cfg["lora_alpha"] / (math.sqrt(cfg["r"]) if cfg.get("use_rslora") else cfg["r"])
            sd = m.load_safetensors_numpy(adapter / "adapter_model.safetensors")
            updates = {}
            import re
            for key, a in sd.items():
                match = re.search(r"layers\\.(\\d+)\\.(self_attn\\.(?:q_proj|k_proj|v_proj|o_proj)|mlp\\.(?:gate_proj|up_proj|down_proj))\\.lora_A\\.weight$", key)
                if match:
                    layer, proj = match.groups()
                    name = f"blk.{layer}.{m.HF_TO_GGUF_SUFFIX[proj]}"
                    updates[name] = (a, sd[key.replace(".lora_A.", ".lora_B.")])
            assert len(updates) == 205 and set(updates).issubset(tensors)
            with base.open("rb") as src:
                src.seek(16)
                count = struct.unpack("<Q", src.read(8))[0]
                alignment = 32
                for _ in range(count):
                    key = m._read_gguf_str(src)
                    typ = struct.unpack("<I", src.read(4))[0]
                    if key == "general.alignment":
                        assert typ == 4
                        alignment = struct.unpack("<I", src.read(4))[0]
                    else:
                        m._skip_gguf_val(src, typ)
                prefix_end = src.tell()
                src.seek(0)
                prefix = src.read(prefix_end)
                layout = []
                relative = 0
                header = bytearray(prefix)
                for name, info in tensors.items():
                    typ = 1 if name in updates else info["ggml_type"]
                    n = math.prod(info["dims"])
                    block, size = m.GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES[typ]
                    length = n // block * size
                    encoded = name.encode()
                    header.extend(struct.pack("<Q", len(encoded)) + encoded)
                    header.extend(struct.pack("<I", len(info["dims"])))
                    header.extend(struct.pack("<" + "Q" * len(info["dims"]), *info["dims"]))
                    header.extend(struct.pack("<IQ", typ, relative))
                    layout.append((name, info, relative, length))
                    relative = (relative + length + alignment - 1) // alignment * alignment
                header.extend(bytes((-len(header)) % alignment))
                with destination.open("wb") as dst:
                    dst.write(header)
                    for i, (name, info, relative, length) in enumerate(layout):
                        dst.seek(len(header) + relative)
                        src.seek(offset + info["rel_offset"])
                        block, size = m.GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES[info["ggml_type"]]
                        raw = src.read(math.prod(info["dims"]) // block * size)
                        if name in updates:
                            assert info["ggml_type"] in (12, 13, 14)
                            a, b = updates[name]
                            assert list((a.shape[1], b.shape[0])) == info["dims"]
                            raw = m.patch_gguf_tensor_bytes(raw, info["ggml_type"], scale * (b @ a), output_f16=True)
                        assert len(raw) == length
                        dst.write(raw)
                        if i % 50 == 0:
                            print(f"Wrote {i + 1}/{len(layout)} tensors", flush=True)
            return {"patched_matrices": len(updates), "merged_tensor_type": "F16", "gguf_path": str(destination)}
        ''',encoding='utf-8')
        '@ | python -

        import sys
        sys.path.insert(0,'.tools/gguf')
        import gguf, numpy as np
        from backend.merge_finetuned_models import *
        p=Path.home()/'.ollama/models/blobs/sha256-e6d47b7c6316612da747c4e471d10f5f1b87c8bf6051332d80f4810d69196c64'
        o,t=parse_gguf_tensors(p)
        with p.open('rb') as f:
         for name in ['blk.0.attn_q.weight','blk.0.attn_v.weight']:
          x=t[name];typ=x['ggml_type'];block,size=GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES[typ];f.seek(o+x['rel_offset']);raw=f.read(32*size)
          expected=gguf.quants.dequantize(np.frombuffer(raw,dtype=np.uint8),gguf.GGMLQuantizationType(typ)).flatten()
          actual=np.frombuffer(patch_gguf_tensor_bytes(raw,typ,np.zeros(32*block,dtype=np.float32),True),dtype='<f2')
          print(name,'decoder max error',np.max(np.abs(actual-expected)),'relative',np.linalg.norm(actual-expected)/np.linalg.norm(expected))
        '@ | python -

        with p.open('rb') as f:
         for name in ['blk.0.attn_q.weight','blk.0.attn_v.weight']:
          x=t[name];typ=x['ggml_type'];block,size=GGML_TYPE_BYTE_SIZES[typ];f.seek(o+x['rel_offset']);raw=f.read(32*size)
          expected=dequantize(np.frombuffer(raw,dtype=np.uint8),GGMLQuantizationType(typ)).flatten()
          actual=np.frombuffer(patch_gguf_tensor_bytes(raw,typ,np.zeros(32*block,dtype=np.float32),True),dtype='<f2')
          print(name,'decoder max error',np.max(np.abs(actual-expected)),'relative',np.linalg.norm(actual-expected)/np.linalg.norm(expected))

    """Rebuild from the original GGUF, retaining merged matrices in F16."""
    import json
    import math
    import struct
    from pathlib import Path
    import numpy as np
    from backend import merge_finetuned_models as m
    
    
    def rebuild(base: Path, adapter: Path, destination: Path):
        offset, tensors = m.parse_gguf_tensors(base)
        cfg = json.loads((adapter / "adapter_config.json").read_text())
        scale = cfg["lora_alpha"] / (math.sqrt(cfg["r"]) if cfg.get("use_rslora") else cfg["r"])
        sd = m.load_safetensors_numpy(adapter / "adapter_model.safetensors")
        updates = {}
